Conversation List\n\n| Field  | Value                 |\n| ------ | --------------------- |\n| Issue  | re-cinq/HALEngine#143 |\n| Status | Shipped               |\n\nResume gets a client back into one conversation: the id it names, or the user's most recently active one. Neither tells it which conversations exist, and every other member of `SessionStore` answers about a session the caller can already name, so a consumer wanting to offer a conversation list had to query the collection behind the engine's back, against a document shape and a key the engine owns. `listFor` answers the list instead, as summaries rather than sessions. **GDPR.** A summary is personal data — that a user held a conversation, and when — so it carries no conversation content, it is filtered to one user by the query rather than in application code afterwards, and the retention bound stays the consumer's, through the store's `maxAgeMs` and erasure methods and re-cinq/HALEngine#41.\n\n## The summary\n\n- A summary is `sessionId`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt` and `entryCount`, and nothing else: neither the conversation's entries nor its credentials, so a list can never hand over either, even for a session created with credentials ([validated by: carries only its four fields, so a list can never hand over a conversation or a credential](../../src/infrastructure/stores/sessionStoreListSignature.test.ts#L22)).\n- `listFor` is optional, like `latestFor` and `lookup` before it: a store written against the five original members still satisfies the interface, and both shipped stores implement it ([validated by: leaves a store with only the five original members satisfying the interface beside both shipped ones](../../src/infrastructure/stores/sessionStoreListSignature.test.ts#L11)).\n\n## The order a list answers in\n\n- Summaries come back most recent activity first ([validated by: answers newest activity first, headed by the very session latestFor returns](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L27)).\n- The first summary is the session `latestFor` returns, because both read the same activity ([validated by: answers newest activity first, headed by the very session latestFor returns](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L27)).\n- The order is total rather than merely sorted: two conversations last active in the same millisecond are ordered by session id, the larger first, which is what lets a page resume without dropping one of them ([validated by: keeps a conversation tied on activity rather than dropping it across a page boundary](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L141)).\n- In `InMemorySessionStore` that tie-break is the list's alone: `latestFor` breaks the same tie by creation order, so for two tied conversations the head of the list and the session a resume rejoins can differ ([validated by: breaks a tie on activity by session id, where latestFor takes the session created later](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L42)).\n- `MongoSessionStore` answers in order of `updatedAt`, which each `save` moves forward, and its first summary is the session its own `latestFor` returns ([validated by: answers the user's conversations newest saved first, headed by the one latestFor returns](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L31)).\n- It breaks a tie by `_id` in both members, so there the head of the list is the session a resume rejoins even when every conversation shares an `updatedAt` ([validated by: heads the list with the session latestFor returns even when every conversation shares an updatedAt](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L161)).\n\n## What a summary reports\n\n- `InMemorySessionStore` reports the moment `create` was called, the newest entry time as the activity, and the conversation's entry count ([validated by: reports a creation time, the newest activity and the entry count of each conversation](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L53)).\n- `MongoSessionStore` reports the document's `createdAt`, the `updatedAt` its last save wrote, and the counter written beside the entries ([validated by: reports the creation time, the last save and the entry count of each conversation](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L49)).\n- That counter is what a save writes, so a list never reads a conversation to count one ([validated by: counts a conversation from the field a save writes beside the entries](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L95)).\n- A document written before the counter existed reads as a conversation of no entries rather than failing the list, which is what the backlog documented in `docs/session-stores.md` is for ([validated by: reads a conversation saved before the counter existed as one of no entries, rather than failing](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L79)).\n\n## Whose conversations\n\n- A list holds only the asking user's conversations: another user's never appear, and a user with none gets an empty list ([validated by: omits a conversation that aged out and another user's, and answers nothing for a user with none](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L65)).\n- `InMemorySessionStore` omits a conversation that has aged out, as `latestFor` does ([validated by: omits a conversation that aged out and another user's, and answers nothing for a user with none](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L65)).\n- `MongoSessionStore` filters on the user in the database, sorts on `{updatedAt: -1, _id: -1}` — the `{userId: 1, updatedAt: -1}` index `latestFor` already needs serves its leading keys — and asks for a projection that leaves the entries behind ([validated by: asks the database to filter on the user, sort on the index with the id behind it, and never for the entries](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L108)).\n- It answers from that projection alone, so a collection that never returns an entry still lists conversations — the conversation does not leave the database to be listed ([validated by: answers from a projection alone, so a collection that never returns entries still lists conversations](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L181)).\n\n## Paging\n\n- `limit` takes that many of the newest, and `before` resumes after a row, so a client pages by passing back the last summary it saw — `SessionCursor` is `updatedAt` and `sessionId`, which a `SessionSummary` already satisfies ([validated by: pages down the same order: a limit takes the newest, and before takes what is older than it](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L78)).\n- Paging walks every conversation exactly once even when all of them share an activity time, because the cursor carries the session id the order breaks ties on: a tie cannot fall between two pages and be skipped ([validated by: keeps a conversation tied on activity rather than dropping it across a page boundary](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L141)).\n- `MongoSessionStore` pages the same way ([validated by: pages with before and a limit, down the same newest-first order](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L62)).\n- It walks a tie exactly once too, through the same cursor ([validated by: keeps a conversation tied on updatedAt rather than dropping it across a page boundary](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L127)).\n- A list is at most 200 summaries however many are asked for, and 50 when none is asked for, so no caller can ask for an unbounded page ([validated by: answers at most two hundred conversations however many are asked for, and fifty when none is](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L94)).\n- That limit bounds the rows answered, not the rows the database reads to answer them: on `MongoSessionStore` the work is bounded by the `{userId: 1, updatedAt: -1, _id: -1}` index `docs/session-stores.md` prescribes, and under the two-key form Mongo sorts the user's whole history before applying the limit ([validated by: needs the three-key index to answer without sorting the whole history, which the two-key form does not](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L192)).\n- A fractional limit is floored rather than refused, since a slip in a limit is not a reason to deny a user their history ([validated by: floors a fractional limit, and reads a negative or unusable one as none given rather than as nothing](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L104)).\n- A negative limit, and one that is not a usable number, are read as no limit given at all rather than as a limit of none: handing a user an empty history for a typo is the worse reading ([validated by: floors a fractional limit, and reads a negative or unusable one as none given rather than as nothing](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L104)).\n- Only a limit of exactly `0` answers no conversations ([validated by: answers nothing only for a limit of exactly none](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L116)).\n- `MongoSessionStore` answers a limit of `0` without querying, because MongoDB reads `limit: 0` as no limit at all and would otherwise answer with every conversation the filter matches ([validated by: answers a limit of none without asking the database, which reads a limit of zero as no limit at all](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L143)).\n\n## The two shipped stores answer one order\n\n- `InMemorySessionStore` and `MongoSessionStore` list the same conversations in the same order, so a consumer swapping one for the other sees the same conversation list ([validated by: answer the same order for conversations whose activity differs](../../src/infrastructure/stores/storeListAgreement.test.ts#L43)).\n- They break a tie on activity the same way, by session id ([validated by: break a tie on activity the same way, by session id](../../src/infrastructure/stores/storeListAgreement.test.ts#L52)).\n- They page identically through the documented call, `listFor(userId, {limit, before: rows.at(-1)})`, a page at a time until a page comes back empty ([validated by: page the same way through the documented call, two rows at a time from the last row seen](../../src/infrastructure/stores/storeListAgreement.test.ts#L61)).\n\n## A cursor the caller can actually hold\n\n- A cursor's `updatedAt` may be a `Date`, an ISO string or an epoch number, so a summary that went through JSON on its way to a client and back still pages ([validated by: pages on a cursor that has been through JSON, and refuses one carrying no usable moment](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L123)).\n- `MongoSessionStore` reads such a cursor as a date before it queries, since a string compares against no stored BSON date and would quietly match none of them ([validated by: pages on a cursor that has been through JSON, where a string would have matched no stored date](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L150)).\n- A cursor carrying no usable moment is refused with an error naming it, rather than read as no cursor at all, which would answer the first page again and repeat rows a client has already shown ([validated by: pages on a cursor that has been through JSON, and refuses one carrying no usable moment](../../src/infrastructure/stores/inMemoryConversationList.test.ts#L123)).\n\n## A collection that cannot list\n\n- `find` is optional on `CollectionLike`, so an adapter written before conversation lists existed still satisfies the port ([validated by: refuses to list on a collection that implements no find, rather than reporting no conversations](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L175)).\n- A store built on a collection without it refuses to list, rather than reporting that the user has no conversations ([validated by: refuses to list on a collection that implements no find, rather than reporting no conversations](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L175)).\n\n## When the store cannot answer\n\n- A store whose collection fails rejects rather than answering a partial or empty list: what to show a user who may have conversations is the caller's decision, and an empty history is not a safe guess ([validated by: rejects rather than answering a partial list when the collection fails](../../src/infrastructure/stores/mongo/mongoConversationList.test.ts#L225)).\n\n## Over the WebSocket protocol\n\n- A client sends `{type: 'list_sessions', limit?, before?}` and receives `{type: 'session_list', sessions}` for the socket's authenticated user, with `createdAt` and `updatedAt` serialised as ISO-8601 strings and any client-supplied `userId` ignored ([validated by: answers list_sessions for the authenticated user with ISO dates, forwarding limit and before while ignoring any client userId](../../src/transport/conversationList.acceptance.test.ts#L87)).\n- A store without `listFor` answers `{type: 'session_list', sessions: []}`, and a throwing `listFor` logs an error on the `ws` category and answers `{type: 'error', code: 'SERVER_ERROR'}` ([validated by: answers an empty session_list when the store has no listFor, and SERVER_ERROR with a ws error log when listFor throws](../../src/transport/conversationList.acceptance.test.ts#L129)).\n- The validator accepts an optional non-negative integer `limit` and an optional `before` cursor whose `sessionId` is 1–128 characters and whose `updatedAt` parses as a valid timestamp, rejecting any other `list_sessions` payload with `INVALID_MESSAGE` without calling `listFor` ([validated by: accepts a bare list_sessions frame and rejects a malformed limit or before with INVALID_MESSAGE without calling listFor](../../src/transport/conversationList.acceptance.test.ts#L161)).\n----\n# Changelog\n\nAll notable changes to `@re-cinq/hal-engine` are documented here.\n\nThe format is [Keep a Changelog 1.1.0](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/), and this project adheres to\n[Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html). Entries are written for somebody installing the\npackage, not for somebody reading this repository's commit log.\n\n## [Unreleased]\n\n### Added\n\n- `SessionStore.listFor(userId, options?)`, an optional store member that answers the summaries of a user's conversations — `sessionId`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt` and `entryCount` — most recent activity first, so a consumer can build a conversation list instead of only rejoining one. Nothing in the engine calls it yet: the store answers the list, and the route or frame that carries it to a client is still yours to write. `InMemorySessionStore` and `MongoSessionStore` both implement it; a store of your own may leave it out. A summary never carries a conversation's entries or its credentials. Page with `limit` (50 by default, 200 at most, and `0` answers nothing) and `before`, which takes the last summary you saw: the cursor carries both `updatedAt` and `sessionId`, so two conversations saved in the same millisecond cannot straddle a page boundary and go unlisted. See `docs/session-stores.md` § Listing a user's conversations.\n\n### Changed\n\n- `MongoSessionStore` now writes an `entryCount` field beside the entries on every save, so a conversation list can count a conversation without reading one. A document saved by an earlier version lists as a conversation of no entries until you backfill it; `docs/session-stores.md` carries the one-line command.\n- `MongoSessionStore.latestFor` now breaks a tie on `updatedAt` by `_id`, so it and `listFor` agree on which conversation is newest when two were saved in the same millisecond. Which of two tied conversations a resume rejoins can therefore change.\n- The index to create for `MongoSessionStore` is now `{userId: 1, updatedAt: -1, _id: -1}`. The two-key form that served `latestFor` cannot satisfy `listFor`'s sort, so Mongo would sort a user's whole history on every page. `docs/session-stores.md` § Latest session has the command.\n- `CollectionLike` gains an optional `find`, and `CursorLike`, `FindManyOptions` and `FindOneOptions` are now exported for anyone writing their own adapter. A collection without `find` still satisfies the type; `listFor` refuses on it rather than reporting that the user has no conversations.\n\n## [0.5.0] - 2026-10-04\n\n**Upgrading from 0.4.x.** One thing is removed, and several things an existing consumer observes change. Check these before you bump:\n\n- Every client must offer `hal.v1` beside its token, `new WebSocket(url, ['hal.v1', token])`. A bare-token offer, accepted with a deprecation warning since 0.3.0, now fails to connect, and `credentialFromSubprotocol` returns `undefined` for it.\n- Closing a socket abandons its turn: the run stops reading from the model and abandons its tool calls, no `stream_end` follows the close, and `afterModelResponse` and `onError` do not fire for it, while `afterSession` still does. A missed heartbeat and `stop()` close sockets too, so drain connections before a deploy.\n- Every tool call now has 30 seconds to settle, a `beforeToolCall` policy included. A tool that legitimately runs longer needs a larger `toolTimeoutMs`, or `0` for the old unbounded wait.\n- `ToolRegistry.execute` no longer throws for a tool name the model invented, or rejects when an executor throws: the model is answered instead. It throws a `TypeError` for an input that is not an object, whatever the tool's schema.\n- Messages in one session are answered one at a time, so a message sent while another is streaming starts after that one's `stream_end`.\n----\n#!/usr/bin/env node\n// Holds every `([validated by: <test name>](path#Lnn))` citation to the test it names.\n//\n// Usage:\n//   node scripts/check-spec-anchor-names.mjs           # report, exit 1 on any finding\n//   node scripts/check-spec-anchor-names.mjs --fix     # repoint each line from the name it carries\n//\n// A line number is the wrong thing to cite: inserting an import above a suite moves every test below\n// it, and the citation silently lands on a different, still-valid declaration. The name is what the\n// author meant, so the name is what the citation records and the line becomes a derived pointer this\n// script repairs. The `#Lnn` stays because the lint rule and the coverage job both index by it, and a\n// citation without one marks the whole file covered rather than one test.\nimport {readFileSync, writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';\nimport {execFileSync} from 'node:child_process';\nimport process from 'node:process';\nimport {dirname, join, normalize} from 'node:path';\nimport {root} from coverage job resolves `./../x` and `/x`\n// to the same file and counts them, while this gate and the drift check would never have read them.\nconst ANY_HREF = /\\[validated by(?:: [^\\]]*)?\\]\\((?<path>[^)#\\s]+)#L\\d+\\)/g;\n// `xit`, `xdescribe` and `.skip` declare a test that never runs, which validates nothing.\nconst DECLARATION = /^\\s*(?:f?it|f?describe|test)\\(\\s*(['\"`])((?:\\\\.|(?!\\1).)*)\\1/;\nconst SKIPPED = /^\\s*(?:x(?:it|describe|test)|(?:it|describe|test)\\.skip)\\(\\s*(['\"`])((?:\\\\.|(?!\\1).)*)\\1/;\nconst TEST_FILE = /\\.(?:test|spec)\\.[cm]?[jt]sx?$/;\n\nconst cache = new Map();\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst fix = args.includes('--fix');\nconst named = args.filter(arg => !arg.startsWith('--'));\nconst findings = [];\nlet checked = 0;\nlet repaired = 0;\n\nfor (const spec of named.length > 0 ? named : specs()) {\n  const source = readFileSync(join(root, spec), 'utf8');\n  const specDir = dirname(spec);\n  let changed = false;\n\n  for (const match of source.matchAll(ANY_HREF)) {\n    if (!match.groups.path.startsWith('../')) {\n      findings.push(`${spec}: href \"${match.groups.path}\" must be relative and start with ../`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const rewritten = source.replace(CITATION, (whole, label, path, line, ...rest) => {\n    const groups = rest[rest.length - 1];\n    const target = normalize(join(specDir, groups.path));\n    if (!TEST_FILE.test(target)) return whole;\n\n    checked += 1;\n    const declarations = declarationsIn(target);\n    if (declarations === null) {\n      findings.push(`${spec}: ${groups.path} does not exist`);\n      return whole;\n    }\n\n    const named = groups.label ? groups.label.slice(2) : null;\n    const atLine = declarations.find(declaration => declaration.line === Number(groups.line));\n\n    const skipped = declarations.find(d => d.skipped && (d.name === named || d.line === Number(groups.line)));\n    if (skipped) {\n      findings.push(`${spec}: cites \"${skipped.name}\" at ${groups.path}#L${skipped.line}, which is skipped and validates nothing`);\n      return whole;\n    }\n\n    if (named === null) {\n      if (!atLine) {\n        findings.push(`${spec}: ${groups.path}#L${groups.line} is not a declaration, so no name can be read from it`);\n '../../types/messages.js';\n\nconst MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH = 10000;\n\ninterface ValidationSuccess {\n  valid: true;\n  data: IncomingMessage;\n}\n\ninterface ValidationFailure {\n  valid: false;\n  error: string;\n}\n\nexport type ValidationResult = ValidationSuccess | ValidationFailure;\n\nexport function validateMessage(payload: unknown): ValidationResult {\n  if (typeof payload !== 'object' hal: HalServer}> {\n  const hal = createServer({\n    app: (() => undefined) as unknown as Express,\n    wsAuth: async () => ({id: 'u1'}),\n    orchestrator: answeringOrchestrator,\n    ...options,\n  });\n  await hal.start(0);\n  servers.push(hal);\n  const {port} = hal.server.address() as AddressInfo;\n  return {url: `ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/hal/ws/c1`, hal};\n}\n\n/** Opens a client that the suite's teardown will close, whether its test passed or failed. */\nexport function connectClient(url: string, token = 't'): WebSocket {\n  const client = new WebSocket(url, [HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL, token]);\n  clients.push(client);\n  return client;\n}\n\n/** A promise a test settles by hand, to hold a stream or a hook at a point of its choosing. */\nexport function deferred(): {until: Promise<void>; release: () => void} {\n  let release = (): void => undefined;\n  const until = new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    release = resolve;\n  });\n  return {until, release};\n}\n\n/** Resolves with every frame type seen up to and including the first `until` frame. */\nexport function framesUntil(client: WebSocket, until: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const seen: string[] = [];\n  return new Promise<string[]>(resolve => {\n    client.on('message', raw => {\n      const {type} = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as {type: string};\n      seen.push(type);\n      if (type === until) resolve(seen);\n    });\n  });\n}\n\n/** Opens a client that asks one question the moment the socket opens, resolving on the first `until` frame. */\nexport function askOnOpen(\n  url: string,\n  {until = 'stream_end', token}: {until?: string; token?: string} = {}\n): {client: WebSocket; finished: Promise<string[]>} {\n  const client = connectClient(url, token);\n  const finished = framesUntil(client, until);\n  client.on('open', () => client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'})));\n  return {client, finished};\n}\n\n/** The session id the client's `connected` frame announces, read after the fact; attach it before any frame arrives. */\nexport function announcedId(client: WebSocket): () => string {\n  let sessionId = '';\n  client.on('message', raw => {\n    const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as {type: string; sessionId?: string};\n    if (frame.type === 'connected') sessionId = frame.sessionId ?? '';\n  });\n  return () => sessionId;\n}\n\n/** Tears every socket and server down, so a red test reports instead of hanging the run. */\nexport async function stopEngines(): Promise<void> {\n  for {validateMessage} from './validation.js';\n\n// One wire name for the client frame: the alias is gone, and the frame the validator returns is the exported shape.\n\ndescribe('the websocket frame validator', () => {\n  it('accepts user_message and returns the frame the exported union describes', () => {\n    const result = validateMessage({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'});\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({valid: true, data: {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}});\n  });\n\n  it('rejects send_message, which is no longer a wire name', () => {\n    const result = validateMessage({type: 'send_message', content: 'hello'});\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({valid: false, error: 'Unknown message type: send_message'});\n  });\n\n  it('accepts a bare list_sessions frame and returns it with no limit or before', () => {\n    const result = validateMessage({type: 'list_sessions'});\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({valid: true, data: {type: 'list_sessions'}});\n  });\n\n  it('drops any field the frame does not define, rather than forwarding it', () => {\n    const result = validateMessage({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello', chatId: 'c1'});\n\n    expect(result).toEqual({valid: true, data: {type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}});\n  });\n});\n","numLines":30,"startLine":1,"totalLines":30}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01Cvdf4SpxJQorx1uuLEzxSE","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

type {IncomingMessage} from '../../types/messages.js';\n\nconst MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH = 10000;\n\ninterface ValidationSuccess {\n  valid: true;\n  data: IncomingMessage;\n}\n\ninterface ValidationFailure {\n  valid: false;\n  error: string;\n}\n\nexport type ValidationResult = ValidationSuccess | ValidationFailure;\n\nexport function validateMessage(payload: unknown): ValidationResult {\n  if (typeof payload !== 'object' || payload === null) {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'Message must be an object'};\n  }\n\n  const message = payload as Record<string, unknown>;\n\n  if (typeof message.type !== 'string') {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'Message must have a type field'};\n  }\n\n  switch (message.type) {\n    case 'user_message':\n      return validateUserMessage(message);\n    case 'ping':\n      return validatePingMessage(message);\n    case 'list_sessions':\n      return {valid: true, data: {type: 'list_sessions'}};\n    default:\n      return {valid: false, error: `Unknown message type: ${message.type}`};\n  }\n}\n\nfunction validateUserMessage(message: Record<string, unknown>): ValidationResult {\n  if (typeof message.content !== 'string') {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'user_message must have content string'};\n  }\n\n  const trimmed = message.content.trim();\n\n  if (trimmed.length === 0) {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'Message content cannot be empty'};\n  }\n\n  if (message.content.length > MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH) {\n    return {valid: false, error: `Message content too long (max ${MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH} chars)`};\n  }\n\n  return {\n    valid: true,\n    data: {\n      type: 'user_message',\n      content: message.content,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction validatePingMessage(message: Record<string, unknown>): ValidationResult {\n  if (typeof message.timestamp !== 'number') {\n    return {valid: false, error: 'ping must have timestamp number'};\n  }\n\n  return {\n    valid: true,\n    data: {\n      type: 'ping',\n      timestamp: message.timestamp,\n    },\n  };\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":31,"oldLines":7,"newStart":31,"newLines":7,"lines":["     case 'ping':","       return validatePingMessage(message);","     case '../infrastructure/stores/inMemorySessionStore.js';\n","originalFile":"import type {AddressInfo} from 'node:net';\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {Express} from 'express';\nimport {createServer} from './createServer.js';\nimport type {HalServer, HalServerOptions} from './createServer.js';\nimport {HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\n\n// Shared by the suites that need a real upgrade over a real socket, which no double can stand in for.\n\nconst servers: Array<{stop: () => Promise<void>}> = [];\nconst clients: WebSocket[] = [];\n\n/** An orchestrator that answers once and stops, which is what commits the turn's assistant entry. */\nexport const answeringOrchestrator: ChatOrchestrator = {\n  processMessage: async () => 'hi',\n  async *processMessageStream(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    yield {type: 'text', text: 'hi'};\n    yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n  },\n};\n\n/** Starts an engine on an ephemeral port and returns the URL a client should open. */\nexport async function startEngine(\n  sessionStore: SessionStore,\n  onDisconnect?: (sessionId: string) => void\n): Promise<string> {\n  const {url} = await startEngineWith({sessionStore, onDisconnect});\n  return url;\n}\n\n/** Starts an engine with whatever a suite overrides, returning the server itself beside the URL a client should open. */\nexport async function startEngineWith(\n  options: Partial<HalServerOptions> & Pick<HalServerOptions, 'sessionStore'>\n): Promise<{url: string; hal: HalServer}> {\n  const hal = createServer({\n    app: (() => undefined) as unknown as Express,\n    wsAuth: async () => ({id: 'u1'}),\n    orchestrator: answeringOrchestrator,\n    ...options,\n  });\n  await hal.start(0);\n  servers.push(hal);\n  const {port} = hal.server.address() as AddressInfo;\n  return {url: `ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/hal/ws/c1`, hal};\n}\n\n/** Opens a client that the suite's teardown will close, whether its test passed or failed. */\nexport function connectClient(url: string, token = 't'): WebSocket {\n  const client = new WebSocket(url, [HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL, token]);\n  clients.push(client);\n  return client;\n}\n\n/** A promise a test settles by hand, to hold a stream or a hook at a point of its choosing. */\nexport function deferred(): {until: Promise<void>; release: () => void} {\n  let release = (): void => undefined;\n  const until = new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    release = resolve;\n  });\n  return {until, release};\n}\n\n/** Resolves with every frame type seen up to and including the first `until` frame. */\nexport function framesUntil(client: WebSocket, until: string): Promise<string[]> {\n  const seen: string[] = [];\n  return new Promise<string[]>(resolve => {\n    client.on('message', raw => {\n      const {type} = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as {type: string};\n      seen.push(type);\n      if (type === until) resolve(seen);\n    });\n  });\n}\n\n/** Opens a client that asks one question the moment the socket opens, resolving on the first `until` frame. */\nexport function askOnOpen(\n  url: string,\n  {until = 'stream_end', token}: {until?: string; token?: string} = {}\n): {client: WebSocket; finished: Promise<string[]>} {\n  const client = connectClient(url, token);\n  const finished = framesUntil(client, until);\n  client.on('open', () => client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'})));\n  return {client, finished};\n}\n\n/** The session id the client's `connected` frame announces, read after the fact; attach it before any frame arrives. */\nexport function announcedId(client: WebSocket): () => string {\n  let sessionId = '';\n  client.on('message', raw => {\n    const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as {type: string; sessionId?: string};\n    if (frame.type === 'connected') sessionId = frame.sessionId ?? '';\n  });\n  return () => sessionId;\n}\n\n/** Tears every socket and server down, so a red test reports instead of hanging the run. */\nexport async function stopEngines(): Promise<void> {\n  for (const client of clients.splice(0)) client.terminate();\n  for (const hal of servers.splice(0)) await hal.stop();\n}\n\n/** The one session a store holds, for asserting what survived a close. */\nexport async function onlySession(store: SessionStore, sessionId: string): Promise<ChatSession | undefined> {\n  return (await store.get(sessionId)) as ChatSession | undefined;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":8,"oldLines":6,"newStart":8,"newLines":7,"lines":[" import type {MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';"," import type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';"," import type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';","+import type {InMemorySessionStore} from '../infrastructure/stores/inMemorySessionStore.js';"," "," // Shared by the suites that need a real upgrade over a real socket, which no double can stand in for."," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"contentNotInModelContext":true},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_013ciGLQAGc8ciJYy8aTJ24J","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

10));\n};\n\n","newString":"","originalFile":"import {once} from 'node:events';\nimport type {AddressInfo} from 'node:net';\nimport {WebSocket} from 'ws';\nimport type {Express} from 'express';\nimport {createServer} from './createServer.js';\nimport {HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL} from './ws/subprotocol.js';\nimport type {SessionResumeOptions} from './ws/sessionResume.js';\nimport {answeringOrchestrator} from './wsTestSupport.js';\nimport {InMemorySessionStore} from '../infrastructure/stores/inMemorySessionStore.js';\nimport type {ChatOrchestrator} from '../orchestration/chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {MessageChunk} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ConnectedMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../types/session.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\nimport {setLogger} from '../shared/logger.js';\n\n// Real upgrades over a real socket: what a reconnect is sent is the contract, and no double can observe it.\n\ninterface Frame {\n  type: string;\n  sessionId?: string;\n  resumed?: boolean;\n  entryCount?: number;\n  resumeFailure?: string;\n  index?: number;\n  entry?: SessionEntry;\n}\n\nconst NEVER_ISSUED = '0f1e2d3c-4b5a-4968-8776-655443322110';\nconst RESUME_ON: SessionResumeOptions = {enabled: true};\nconst LATEST_ON: SessionResumeOptions = {enabled: true, latest: true};\nconst HALF_ANSWER: SessionEntry = {\n  role: 'assistant',\n  content: 'half',\n  timestamp: '2026-09-30T08:00:01.000Z',\n  isStreaming: true,\n};\n\n// A tool's suppression lands while the answer is still open, as when a provider ends a tool round with no stop.\nconst suppressingMidAnswer: ChatOrchestrator = {\n  processMessage: async () => 'before after',\n  async *processMessageStream(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n    yield {type: 'text', text: 'before'};\n    yield {type: 'suppress_output'} as unknown as MessageChunk;\n    yield {type: 'text', text: ' after'};\n    yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n  },\n};\n\nconst servers: Array<ReturnType<typeof createServer>> = [];\nconst clients: WebSocket[] = [];\n\nconst eventually = async (check: () => boolean): Promise<void> => {\n  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 50 && !check(); attempt++) await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 10));\n};\n\n// Silenced for the whole file: the server logs every connection and close, and these tests read frames, not logs.\nbeforeAll(() => {\n  setLogger({debug: () => undefined, info: () => undefined, warn: () => undefined, error: () => undefined});\n});\n\nafterAll(() => {\n  setLogger();\n});\n\n// Each server sees its sockets close before it stops, so no close is logged after the logger above is restored.\nafterEach(async () => {\n  for (const client of clients.splice(0)) client.terminate();\n  for (const hal of servers.splice(0)) {\n    const {wss} = hal;\n    await eventually(() => wss.clients.size === 0);\n    await hal.stop();\n  }\n});\n\n// The authenticated user is read per upgrade, so one test can reconnect as someone else.\nconst startEngine = async (\n  store: SessionStore,\n  resume?: SessionResumeOptions,\n  orchestrator: ChatOrchestrator = answeringOrchestrator\n) => {\n  const auth = {userId: 'u1'};\n  const connects: Array<{sessionId: string; resumed: boolean}> = [];\n  const disconnected: string[] = [];\n  const hal = createServer({\n    app: (() => undefined) as unknown as Express,\n    wsAuth: async () => ({id: auth.userId}),\n    sessionStore: store,\n    orchestrator,\n    resume,\n    onConnect: (session, {resumed}) => void connects.push({sessionId: session.sessionId, resumed}),\n    onDisconnect: sessionId => void disconnected.push(sessionId),\n  });\n  // Resolves once the server itself has seen its first socket close, which the client's own close can precede.\n  const firstServerClose = new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    hal.wss.once('connection', socket => socket.once('close', () => resolve()));\n  });\n  await hal.start(0);\n  servers.push(hal);\n  const {port} = hal.server.address() as AddressInfo;\n  return {auth, connects, disconnected, firstServerClose, base: `ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/hal/ws`};\n};\n\nconst open = (url: string, token = 't'): WebSocket => {\n  const client = new WebSocket(url, [HAL_WS_SUBPROTOCOL, token]);\n  clients.push(client);\n  return client;\n};\n\n// One question answered and the socket closed, returning the id the connected frame announced.\nconst firstTurn = async (base: string): Promise<string> => {\n  const client = open(base);\n  let sessionId = '';\n  await new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    client.on('message', raw => {\n      const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as Frame;\n      if (frame.type === 'connected') sessionId = frame.sessionId ?? '';\n      if (frame.type === 'connected') client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'user_message', content: 'hello'}));\n      if (frame.type === 'stream_end') resolve();\n    });\n  });\n  client.close();\n  await once(client, 'close');\n  return sessionId;\n};\n\n// A ping sent on connect is a barrier: its pong can only follow every frame the replay wrote.\nconst connectAndSettle = async (url: string, token?: string): Promise<Frame[]> => {\n  const client = open(url, token);\n  const frames: Frame[] = [];\n  await new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    client.on('message', raw => {\n      const frame = JSON.parse(String(raw)) as Frame;\n      frames.push(frame);\n      if (frame.type === 'connected') client.send(JSON.stringify({type: 'ping', timestamp: 1}));\n      if (frame.type === 'pong') resolve();\n    });\n  });\n  return frames;\n};\n\nconst upserts = (frames: Frame[]) =>\n  frames.filter(frame => frame.type === 'entry_upsert').map(({index, entry}) => ({index, entry}));\n\nconst shapeOf = (frames: Frame[]) => ({\n  keys: Object.keys(frames[0]).sort(),\n  resumed: frames[0].resumed,\n  resumeFailure: frames[0].resumeFailure,\n  types: frames.map(frame => frame.type),\n});\n\nconst deferredRelease = () => {\n  let release = (): void => undefined;\n  const until = new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n    release = resolve;\n  });\n  return {until, release: () => release()};\n};\n\n// Each stored conversation holds one user entry, sent at the given time and reading as its own id.\nconst storeWith = (conversations: Array<[sessionId: string, userId: string, sentAt: string]>): InMemorySessionStore => {\n  const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n  for (const [sessionId, userId, sentAt] of conversations) {\n    const {entries} = store.create(sessionId, userId);\n    entries.push({role: 'user', content: sessionId, timestamp: sentAt});\n  }\n  return store;\n};\n\n// A store with only the required members, so an optional one can be added, replaced or left out per test.\nconst delegatingTo = (inner: InMemorySessionStore): SessionStore => ({\n  create: (sessionId, userId, options) => inner.create(sessionId, userId, options),\n  get: sessionId => inner.get(sessionId),\n  delete: sessionId => inner.delete(sessionId),\n  count: () => inner.count(),\n  clear: () => inner.clear(),\n});\n\ndescribe('resuming a conversation on reconnect', () => {\n  it('with resume absent, answers a reconnect naming the first id with a new id and no replay', async () => {\n    const {base} = await startEngine(new InMemorySessionStore());\n    const first = await firstTurn(base);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${first}`);\n\n    expect({newId: frames[0].sessionId !== first, shape: shapeOf(frames)}).toEqual({\n      newId: true,\n      shape: {\n        keys: ['examplePrompts', 'message', 'sessionId', 'type'],\n        resumed: undefined,\n        types: ['connected', 'pong'],\n      },\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('with resume on, answers the owner reconnecting with resumed true, entryCount 2 and both stored entries', async () => {\n    const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const {base} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n    const first = await firstTurn(base);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${first}`);\n\n    const stored = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify((await store.get(first))?.entries)) as SessionEntry[];\n    expect({connected: frames[0], replay: upserts(frames)}).toMatchObject({\n      connected: {type: 'connected', sessionId: first, resumed: true, entryCount: 2},\n      replay: [\n        {index: 0, entry: stored[0]},\n        {index: 1, entry: stored[1]},\n      ],\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('answers another user naming the id exactly as it answers an id that was never issued', async () => {\n    const {auth, base} = await startEngine(new InMemorySessionStore(), RESUME_ON);\n    const first = await firstTurn(base);\n    auth.userId = 'u2';\n\n    const otherUser = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${first}`);\n    const neverIssued = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${NEVER_ISSUED}`);\n\n    expect({fresh: otherUser[0].sessionId !== first, otherUser: shapeOf(otherUser)}).toEqual({\n      fresh: true,\n      otherUser: shapeOf(neverIssued),\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('answers a never-issued id with resumed false, resumeFailure unknown, and no replay or error', async () => {\n    const {base} = await startEngine(new InMemorySessionStore(), RESUME_ON);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${NEVER_ISSUED}`);\n\n    expect(shapeOf(frames)).toEqual({\n      keys: ['examplePrompts', 'message', 'resumeFailure', 'resumed', 'sessionId', 'type'],\n      resumed: false,\n      resumeFailure: 'unknown',\n      types: ['connected', 'pong'],\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('never adopts a requested id as a key: one session, under the id the server minted', async () => {\n    const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const {base} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${NEVER_ISSUED}`);\n\n    expect({\n      held: await store.count(),\n      adopted: (await store.get(NEVER_ISSUED)) !== undefined,\n      minted: (await store.get(frames[0].sessionId ?? '')) !== undefined,\n    }).toEqual({held: 1, adopted: false, minted: true});\n  });\n\n  it('still calls onDisconnect with the session id when a socket closes under resume', async () => {\n    const {disconnected, base} = await startEngine(new InMemorySessionStore(), RESUME_ON);\n\n    const first = await firstTurn(base);\n    await eventually(() => disconnected.length > 0);\n\n    expect(disconnected).toEqual([first]);\n  });\n\n  it('keeps the credentials of the socket that resumed a session when the socket it replaced closes', async () => {\n    const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const {base, disconnected, firstServerClose} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n    const replaced = open(base, 'old');\n    const [greeting] = (await once(replaced, 'message')) as [Buffer];\n    const sessionId = (JSON.parse(String(greeting)) as Frame).sessionId ?? '';\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${sessionId}`, 'new');\n    replaced.close();\n    await firstServerClose;\n\n    const held = await store.get(sessionId);\n    expect({resumed: frames[0].resumed, disconnected, authorization: held?.authHeaders?.authorization}).toEqual({\n      resumed: true,\n      disconnected: [sessionId],\n      authorization: 'Bearer new',\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('gives a session back the credentials of a socket still open when the socket that resumed it closes', async () => {\n    const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const {base} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n    const first = open(base, 'first');\n    const [greeting] = (await once(first, 'message')) as [Buffer];\n    const sessionId = (JSON.parse(String(greeting)) as Frame).sessionId ?? '';\n    const second = open(`${base}?sessionId=${sessionId}`, 'second');\n    await once(second, 'message');\n    const held = await store.get(sessionId);\n    const whileBothOpen = held?.authHeaders?.authorization;\n\n    second.close();\n    await eventually(() => held?.authHeaders?.authorization !== whileBothOpen);\n\n    expect({whileBothOpen, afterSecondCloses: held?.authHeaders?.authorization}).toEqual({\n      whileBothOpen: 'Bearer second',\n      afterSecondCloses: 'Bearer first',\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('tells onConnect whether a connection started its session or resumed it', async () => {\n    const {base, connects} = await startEngine(new InMemorySessionStore(), RESUME_ON);\n    const first = await firstTurn(base);\n\n    await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${first}`);\n\n    expect(connects).toEqual([\n      {sessionId: first, resumed: false},\n      {sessionId: first, resumed: true},\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it('resumes the id its store knows and starts fresh for one it does not', async () => {\n    const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const known = await store.create('known', 'u1');\n    known.entries.push({role: 'user', content: 'earlier', timestamp: '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z'});\n    const {base} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n\n    const resumed = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=known`);\n    const fresh = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=unknown`);\n\n    expect({resumed: resumed[0], fresh: fresh[0]}).toMatchObject({\n      resumed: {sessionId: 'known', resumed: true, entryCount: 1},\n      fresh: {resumed: false},\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('replays a suppressed entry as a skip, so the client never sees text a tool suppressed', async () => {\n    const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const known = await store.create('known', 'u1');\n    known.entries.push(\n      {role: 'user', content: 'hello', timestamp: '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z'},\n      {\n        role: 'assistant',\n        content: 'hidden',\n        timestamp: '2026-09-30T08:00:01.000Z',\n        isStreaming: false,\n        suppressed: true,\n      }\n    );\n    const {base} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=known`);\n\n    expect({\n      types: frames.map(({type, index}) => (index === undefined ? type : `${type} ${index}`)),\n      leaked: JSON.stringify(frames).includes('hidden'),\n    }).toEqual({types: ['connected', 'entry_upsert 0', 'entry_skip 1', 'pong'], leaked: false});\n  });\n\n  it('replays an answer suppressed while it was still open as skips, as blank as the live client was left', async () => {\n    const {base} = await startEngine(new InMemorySessionStore(), RESUME_ON, suppressingMidAnswer);\n    const first = await firstTurn(base);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${first}`);\n\n    const replayed = JSON.stringify(frames);\n    expect({\n      types: frames.map(({type, index}) => (index === undefined ? type : `${type} ${index}`)),\n      leaked: ['before', 'after'].filter(word => replayed.includes(word)),\n    }).toEqual({types: ['connected', 'entry_upsert 0', 'entry_skip 1', 'entry_skip 2', 'pong'], leaked: []});\n  });\n\n  it('replays an entry whose turn is still running as finished and truncated, leaving the stored entry as it is', async () => {\n    const store = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const known = await store.create('known', 'u1');\n    known.entries.push({role: 'user', content: 'hello', timestamp: '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z'}, {...HALF_ANSWER});\n    const {base} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=known`);\n\n    expect({replayed: upserts(frames)[1], stored: known.entries[1]}).toEqual({\n      replayed: {index: 1, entry: {...HALF_ANSWER, isStreaming: false, truncated: true}},\n      stored: HALF_ANSWER,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('keeps a resumed session whose socket closed before the replay, rather than evicting it', async () => {\n    const inner = new InMemorySessionStore();\n    const known = await inner.create('known', 'u1');\n    known.entries.push({role: 'user', content: 'earlier', timestamp: '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z'});\n    const lookupHeld = deferredRelease();\n    const evicted: string[] = [];\n    const store: SessionStore = {\n      create: (sessionId, userId, options) => inner.create(sessionId, userId, options),\n      get: async sessionId => {\n        await lookupHeld.until;\n        return inner.get(sessionId);\n      },\n      delete: sessionId => inner.delete(sessionId),\n      count: () => inner.count(),\n      clear: () => inner.clear(),\n      evict: sessionId => {\n        evicted.push(sessionId);\n        return inner.evict(sessionId);\n      },\n    };\n    const {base, firstServerClose} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n\n    const client = open(`${base}?sessionId=known`);\n    await once(client, 'open');\n    // Terminated, not closed: the server holds the socket paused across the lookup, so it never reads a close frame.\n    client.terminate();\n    await firstServerClose;\n    lookupHeld.release();\n    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 50));\n\n    const kept = inner.get('known');\n    expect({evicted, kept: kept?.entries.length}).toEqual({evicted: [], kept: 1});\n  });\n\n  it('with resume off, still completes a handshake at /hal/ws/undefined and mints an id', async () => {\n    const {base} = await startEngine(new InMemorySessionStore());\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}/undefined`);\n\n    expect(frames[0]).toMatchObject({type: 'connected', sessionId: expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/)});\n  });\n\n  it('still types a connected frame literal that carries neither resume field', () => {\n    const legacy: ConnectedMessage = {type: 'connected', sessionId: 's1', message: 'hi', examplePrompts: []};\n\n    expect(Object.keys(legacy)).toEqual(['type', 'sessionId', 'message', 'examplePrompts']);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('continuing the latest session', () => {\n  it('with latest on, rejoins the most recently active session of a user whose connect names none', async () => {\n    const store = storeWith([\n      ['older', 'u1', '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z'],\n      ['newer', 'u1', '2026-09-30T09:00:00.000Z'],\n      ['theirs', 'u2', '2026-09-30T10:00:00.000Z'],\n    ]);\n    const {base, connects} = await startEngine(store, LATEST_ON);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(base);\n\n    expect({connected: frames[0], replay: upserts(frames), connects}).toMatchObject({\n      connected: {sessionId: 'newer', resumed: true, entryCount: 1},\n      replay: [{index: 0, entry: {role: 'user', content: 'newer'}}],\n      connects: [{sessionId: 'newer', resumed: true}],\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('with latest on, starts a new session for a user with none, and on ?new=1 even when one exists', async () => {\n    const store = storeWith([['known', 'u1', '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z']]);\n    const {auth, base} = await startEngine(store, LATEST_ON);\n\n    const asked = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?new=1`);\n    auth.userId = 'u2';\n    const firstVisit = await connectAndSettle(base);\n\n    expect({\n      asked: {resumed: asked[0].resumed, minted: asked[0].sessionId !== 'known'},\n      firstVisit: {resumed: firstVisit[0].resumed, minted: firstVisit[0].sessionId !== 'known'},\n      held: store.count(),\n    }).toEqual({asked: {resumed: false, minted: true}, firstVisit: {resumed: false, minted: true}, held: 3});\n  });\n\n  it('with latest on, still rejoins the session a connect names rather than the latest one', async () => {\n    const store = storeWith([\n      ['older', 'u1', '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z'],\n      ['newer', 'u1', '2026-09-30T09:00:00.000Z'],\n    ]);\n    const {base} = await startEngine(store, LATEST_ON);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=older`);\n\n    expect(frames[0]).toMatchObject({sessionId: 'older', resumed: true});\n  });\n\n  it(\"starts a new session when the store's latestFor answers another user's session, throws, or is absent\", async () => {\n    const inner = storeWith([['theirs', 'u2', '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z']]);\n    const stores: Record<string, SessionStore> = {\n      stranger: {...delegatingTo(inner), latestFor: () => inner.get('theirs')},\n      failing: {\n        ...delegatingTo(inner),\n        latestFor: () => {\n          throw new Error('database down');\n        },\n      },\n      absent: delegatingTo(inner),\n    };\n\n    const outcomes: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n    for (const [name, store] of Object.entries(stores)) {\n      const {base} = await startEngine(store, LATEST_ON);\n      const [connected] = await connectAndSettle(base);\n      outcomes[name] = {resumed: connected.resumed, minted: connected.sessionId !== 'theirs'};\n    }\n\n    const fresh = {resumed: false, minted: true};\n    expect(outcomes).toEqual({stranger: fresh, failing: fresh, absent: fresh});\n  });\n\n  it('with resume on but latest off, starts a new session for a connect that names none', async () => {\n    const store = storeWith([['known', 'u1', '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z']]);\n    const {base} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n\n    const [connected] = await connectAndSettle(base);\n\n    expect({resumed: connected.resumed, minted: connected.sessionId !== 'known'}).toEqual({\n      resumed: false,\n      minted: true,\n    });\n  });\n});\n\n// A store whose only session, the one u1 had, outlived its one-millisecond lifetime before anyone asked for it.\nconst agedOut = async (): Promise<InMemorySessionStore> => {\n  const store = new InMemorySessionStore({maxAgeMs: 1});\n  const {entries} = store.create('known', 'u1');\n  entries.push({role: 'user', content: 'my booking 4711', timestamp: '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z'});\n  await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 20));\n  return store;\n};\n\ndescribe('telling an expired session apart', () => {\n  it('tells the owner of a session that aged out that it expired, with a new id and no replay', async () => {\n    const {base} = await startEngine(await agedOut(), RESUME_ON);\n\n    const frames = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=known`);\n\n    expect({\n      connected: frames[0],\n      minted: frames[0].sessionId !== 'known',\n      replayed: upserts(frames).length,\n    }).toMatchObject({\n      connected: {resumed: false, resumeFailure: 'expired'},\n      minted: true,\n      replayed: 0,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('answers another user naming an expired id exactly as it answers an id never issued', async () => {\n    const {auth, base} = await startEngine(await agedOut(), RESUME_ON);\n    auth.userId = 'u2';\n\n    const expired = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=known`);\n    const neverIssued = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${NEVER_ISSUED}`);\n\n    expect({expired: shapeOf(expired), failure: expired[0].resumeFailure}).toEqual({\n      expired: shapeOf(neverIssued),\n      failure: 'unknown',\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('sends no resumeFailure at all when the connect names no id, even with no latest session to rejoin', async () => {\n    const {base} = await startEngine(new InMemorySessionStore(), LATEST_ON);\n\n    const [connected] = await connectAndSettle(base);\n\n    expect({resumed: connected.resumed, failureKey: 'resumeFailure' in connected}).toEqual({\n      resumed: false,\n      failureKey: false,\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('reads a named id with one call per attempt: lookup when the store has it, get otherwise', async () => {\n    const inner = storeWith([['known', 'u1', '2026-09-30T08:00:00.000Z']]);\n    const calls: string[] = [];\n    const fiveMembers: SessionStore = {\n      ...delegatingTo(inner),\n      get: sessionId => {\n        calls.push('get');\n        return inner.get(sessionId);\n      },\n    };\n    const withLookup: SessionStore = {\n      ...fiveMembers,\n      lookup: sessionId => {\n        calls.push('lookup');\n        return inner.lookup(sessionId);\n      },\n    };\n\n    const outcomes: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n    for (const [name, store] of Object.entries({fiveMembers, withLookup})) {\n      const {base} = await startEngine(store, RESUME_ON);\n      const [live] = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=known`);\n      const [dead] = await connectAndSettle(`${base}?sessionId=${NEVER_ISSUED}`);\n      outcomes[name] = {live: live.resumed, dead: dead.resumeFailure};\n    }\n\n    const answered = {live: true, dead: 'unknown'};\n    expect({outcomes, calls}).toEqual({\n      outcomes: {fiveMembers: answered, withLookup: answered},\n      calls: ['get', 'get', 'lookup', 'lookup'],\n    });\n  });\n});\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":50,"oldLines":10,"newStart":50,"newLines":6,"lines":[" const servers: Array<ReturnType<typeof createServer>> = [];"," const clients: WebSocket[] = [];","
